 Ingestor ignores this field for sites with existing client division person (CDP) records.
 While creating CDP records, the Medidata platform considers all entered addresses as Main addresses.
 When updating CDP records, you can add or remove non-Main addresses.
 | Primary Phone 
 | The team member's primary phone number. 
 | Required 
 | String 
 | N/A 
 | 
 The Medidata platform marks the value entered here as the primary phone number and retains whatever uses type that appears at the client division person (CDP) level.
 Note : A CDP's primary phone can differ between the CDP and study environment person (SEP) levels, but the phone number must exist at the CDP level.
 | Other Phones 
 | The team member's alternative phone numbers. 
 | Optional 
 | String 
 | N/A 
 | Ingestor processes all phone numbers entered into this field as non-primary. 
 You can enter phone numbers in one of the following formats:
- Phone Number only – The phone number only, such as 07956463607 . 
- Phone Number with Usage Type – The phone number with the user usage type, such as Home::23459875023. While optional, you can enter the phone numbers' usage type by adding two colons '::' before the phone number. 
 You can enter multiple phone numbers by separating each number with a pipe delimiter '|', for example:
- Phone Number only – 07956463607|23459875023 
- Phone Number with Usage Type – Fax::+44 (097) 40393-82|Mobile::07956463607|Home::23459875023 
 If you do not specify usage types the Medidata platform attempts to link the phone number with a type based on what appears in the CDP record.
 You cannot set a phone number as both a primary phone number and an alternative phone number at the same time.
 | Other Emails 
 | The team member's alternative email addresses. 
 | Optional 
 | String 
 | N/A 
 | Ingestor processes all email addresses entered into this field as non-primary.
 You can enter email addresses in one of the following formats:
- Email Address only – The email address only, such as emails@internet.com . 
- Email Address with Usage Type – The email address with the user usage type, such as Personal::anemail@website.net . While optional, you can enter the phone numbers' usage type by adding two colons '::' before the phone number. 
 You can enter multiple phone numbers by separating each number with a pipe delimiter '|', for example:
- Email Address only – anemail@website.net|emails@internet.com 
- Email Address with Usage Type – Personal::anemail@website.net|Other::emails@internet.com 
 If you do not specify usage types the Medidata platform attempts to link the email address with a type based on what appears in the CDP record.
 You cannot set a phone number as both an email address and an alternative email address at the same time.

===== PAGE: support_import_of_large_files.html | Support Import of Large Files =====
## Support Import of Large Files
## Description
 MEDS Ingestor now supports large file imports. Testing included importing files with more than one million rows of data. 
 Feature/enhancement stories referenced in this article:
 MCC-730459, MCC-727227 
## Affected Roles
 All Data Import roles.
## Advantages
 The new internal design allows data migration into MEDS on a larger scale than supported in earlier releases.
## User Impact
 None.
## Special Considerations
 Uploading very large files from the user desktop can take several minutes.

===== PAGE: support_multiple_file_import.html | Support for Multiple File Import =====
## Support for Multiple File Import
## Description
 You can upload up to ten comma-separated value (CSV) files in a single import job. MEDS Ingestor validates each file to ensure file names are in the following format: <DatasetOID>-<filename>.csv. Uploads fail when file names do not adhere to the naming convention.  
 Feature/enhancement stories referenced in this article:
 MCC-568549, MCC-568554, MCC-568559, MCC-588678 
## Affected Roles
 All.
## Advantages
 The ability to upload multiple CSV files:
- reduces time spent uploading files. 
- provides a simplified and more intuitive user experience. 
## User Impact
- You can queue up to 10 files for import in the same job, reducing time spent uploading files. 
- File name validation standardizes file names, ensuring that your files import successfully. 
- File name validation occurs when you either  drag-and-drop files onto corresponding import jobs in the Import Summary or c lick Import on a given import job. 
## Special Considerations
 None.

===== PAGE: track_job_progress.html | Track Job Progress =====
## Track Job Progress
 After starting the file import process, you can track the status of your files from the import job's Job Files screen. The Job Files screen shows data related to the files you uploaded. The tracked data differs depending on the type of import job.  You can perform one of the following actions: 
## Open Job Files Table
 You can reach the Job Files screen from an import job.
- Log into iMedidata.com . 
- Navigate to Ingestor through your assigned client division. 
- Identify the import job to review, and click the import job.
 Tip : You can filter import jobs by different parameters, such as Study, Job Type, and Environment. To filter the import job list, click the Filter icon  for one of the parameters, and select which data to filter by.
## Job Files Table Data
 The Job Files screen shows a table listing the various attributes pertaining to all files of the import job.
 Job Report 
 Opens the Import Summary panel. The Import Summary panel describes details about the individual processing jobs. 
 Archive File 
 Indicates if the uploaded file is part of a compressed file. Hovering over shows the name of the compressed file.
 File Name 
 Shows the name of the file uploaded in the processing job. If job processed multiple files, shows a count of the files uploaded in the processing job. For successful jobs, you can expand a column entry to see a list of the imported files and the respective data set names. 
 Data Set Name 
 Shows the name of the uploaded file's data set . 
 Status 
 The Import Summary provides more information about each stage of the data import process.
 Color-coded bars show the progress of data file upload, import, and validation. Errors at any stage cause the import to fail.
 Ingestor displays the current process stage with appropriate colors and text updates.
 Start Time 
 The import job's start time. Ingestor stores the import time as UTC. The time appears in your local timezone.
 End Time 
 The import job's completion time.  Ingestor stores the completion time as UTC. The time appears in your local timezone.
 Imported by 
 The name of the user who started the import.
 Import Method 
 Shows the processing job's upload method: 
- User Upload   – Files uploaded using the Ingestor user interface. 
- SFTP   – Files uploaded using SFTP. 
- API   – Files uploaded using API endpoints. 
 No. of Records 
 Shows the number of records processed by the job .
## Refresh Job Files Data
 You can refresh the Job Files screen to track the progress of jobs that are in progress.
- Click the Refresh icon to reload the Job Files screen. 
## Review Import Summary
 Once an import job finishes with either a success or failed state, you can review the Import Summary.
- For completed jobs, click Job Report  > View  to open the Import Summary panel 
## Import Summary Panel Data
 In the Import Summary panel, you can review a 
 summary of the imported files for a given file import process, or processing job, in one view. The panel shows the files' details, processing summary, and any downloadable artifacts related to the import. 
 General Summary 
 Lists general information about the imported files:
- Study – The target study for the processing job.
 Note : This field only appears for Study Design Metadata jobs and External EDC jobs.
- Study Environment – The target study environment for the processing job. 
- Start Time – The file import's start time in the DD-MMM-YYYY hh:mm:ss format. Start time appears in your local time.  
- End Time – The file import's end time in the DD-MMM-YYYY hh:mm:ss format. Start time appears in your local time.  
- Imported By – Shows the user who imported the files. 
- Run ID – The processing job's identification number. Unique to each processing job for a given import job. You can click the Copy button to save the details on your clipboard. 
- Job ID – The import job's identification number. Unique to each import job. You can click the Copy button to save the details on your clipboard. 
- Workflow Plan ID – The processing job's workflow plan identifier. Unique to each workflow plan for a given processing job. You can click the Copy button to save the details on your clipboard. 
- Workflow Execution ID – The workflow execution's identification number. Unique to each workflow execution job. You can click the Copy button to save the details on your clipboard. 
- Data Integration – The name of data integration name pertaining to the uploaded file.
 Note : This field only appears for external EDC jobs.
- Data Integration Version – The data integrations version pertaining to the uploaded file. If a new data integration version exists, you can update the data integration version by clicking Update .
 Note : This field only appears for external EDC jobs.
- Import Type – Denotes whether the processing job is Cumulative, replacing all existing data; or Incremental, adding to or updating existing data. Processing jobs with the incremental import type can Upsert – add or update existing items – or Delete items.  The uploaded file's name determines if Ingestor upserts or deletes items. If the file's name contains "upsert", then upsertion occurs. If the file's name contains "delete", then deletion occurs. 
 Note :
- An uploaded file can only include "upsert" or "delete", and not both. 
- This field is only available for external EDC jobs. 
 File Upload 
 Shows the job's overall file import status, and lists every file uploaded with the import job, with each file's status:
- Upload Status – Denotes if the file successfully uploaded to Ingestor.  
- File Name – The uploaded file's name. 
- Size – The uploaded file's side. 
- Dataset – The name of the data set the file pertains to. 
- Download – You can download the files uploaded during that job run. 
 Data Validation 
 Shows the job's overall data validation status. If a file fails validation, you can download error files or related artifacts. 
 Data Processing 
 Shows the job's overall data processing status, and lists each file's data processing status with the corresponding record count. Possible statuses:
- Total – The number of rows in the imported file. 
- Imported Without Errors or Warnings – The number of data rows imported without error or warning. 
- New – The number of rows that resulted in created data. This field can appear as "Succeeded" for some data types.  
- Updated – The number of rows that resulted in updated data. 
- Deleted – The number of rows that resulted in deleted data. 
- Processed – The number of rows in the imported file processed by Ingestor. 
- Partially Updated  – The number of rows in the imported file that Ingestor partially loaded, for example: Ingestor successfully loaded some columns and failed on others. 
- Error – The number of processed rows with errors. 
- Reprocessed – The number of rows that required reprocessing. 
- No Changes – The number of rows that resulted in no data changes. 
- Succeeded – The number of rows that resulted in created data. This field can appear as "New" for some data types. 
- Failed – The number of rows that Ingestor could not create, update, or delete.  
- Completed Successfully   – Ingestor finished processing and publishing all records to the Medidata platform; the data are available to access from the Medidata platform. 
- Completed with Failed Rows   – One of the following:
- Ingestor failed to publish some records due to issues on one or more rows of the file. The other successful records are accessible from the Medidata platform. The job's artifact records contain details about any errors. 
- Ingestor published the records successfully, but failed to do so within 72 hours. Successful records are not yet accessible from the Medidata platform. The job's artifact records contains about any errors. 
- Data Processing Failed   – One of the following:
- Ingestor failed to publish the data due to errors in the file. The artifact file contains details about any errors in the file. 
- Ingestor published the records successfully, but failed to do so within 72 hours due to issues with data streaming. The job's artifact records contains about any data flow errors. 
 SFTP 
 The job's SFTP configuration details:
- Location Name  – The name of the SFTP location used by the processing job. 
- Upload Files Path – The extension of the path in the SFTP location where Ingestor imports data files from. 
- Output Files Path – The extension of the path in the SFTP location where Ingestor saves process files. 
 Artifact Files 
 If there are errors, you can also download error lists or reconciliation reports. Reconciliation reports contain details about the data validation and data processing, including error details for each row. 
## Download Imported Files
 For completed processing jobs, you can download the files pertaining to that processing job. You can download imported files regardless of the processing job's success.
- From the Import Summary panel > File Upload section, click  Download for each file you want to download. 
## Next Step
 Manage Job Configurations

===== PAGE: update_job_configuration_details.html | Update Job Configuration Details =====
## Update Job Configuration Details
 After creating one or more import jobs , you can use Ingestor to update job details on each job's Job Configuration screen. Editable job details include the job name and completion status of the job. For some job types, such as external EDC jobs, you can edit additional details. 
 Important : To update job configuration details, you must have a building block  that allows you to Manage Data assigned to your customer-defined platform role. For details, see  Ingestor Roles and Permissions List .
## Open Job Configuration Page
 You can access the Job Configuration page from specific job.
- Log into iMedidata.com . 
- Navigate to Ingestor through an assigned client division. 
- Select an import job to review.
 Tip : You can filter import jobs by different parameters, such as Study, Job Type, and Environment. To filter the import job list, click the Filter icon  for one of the parameters, and select which data to filter by.
- Click the Job Configuration tab.
 Tip : You can also access the Job Configuration page by clicking the gear icon from the jobs list.
## Update Job Name
 You can update the job name to change how the job appears throughout Ingestor.
- On the Job Configuration page, click  Edit Job . 
- Enter the job name. 
- Click  Save Changes . 
## Update Job Completion Status
 You can inactivate jobs by marking them as Complete, or reactivate jobs by marking completed jobs as Active.  When you mark a job complete, you cannot: 
- Import additional data into completed import jobs. 
- Change the associated data integration version associated with completed import jobs. 
## Mark Job As Complete
- On the Job Configuration page, click  Mark as Complete . 
## Mark Job As Active
- On the Job Configuration page, click  Mark as Active . 
## Update Data Integration Fields
 You can update an external EDC job's data integration version. The Job Configuration screen notifies you when a new data integration version is available. You can also lock a data integration version to prevent updates.
## Edit Data Integration Version
- On the Job Configuration page, click  Edit Job . 
- Select a data integration version. 
- Click Save Changes . 
## Lock Data Integration Version
- On the Job Configuration page, click  Lock Version . 
## Update Import Settings
 You can update a job's import settings, such as how it uses SFTP to import data or its import type.
## Toggle Email Notifications
 You can configure import jobs to send email notifications on job completion. The notifications inform your stakeholders if the job completed successfully, successfully with failed rows, or unsuccessfully and includes relevant details. Email notifications deliver for jobs leveraging any import method: through the Ingestor interface, through an external sFTP location, or through Medidata API resources.
 Email notifications trigger for the following users that create jobs, modify jobs, or import files.
- On the Job Configuration page, click  Edit Job . 
- Toggle Receive Email Notification After Job Completion on. 
- Click Save Changes . 
 Toggle SFTP Settings 
 You can enable or disable the job's SFTP settings. When enabling SFTP settings, y ou must define an input folder path to receive files from and an output folder path to place Ingestor's post-processing files. Every 30 minutes, Ingestor imports the files from the Input File Path and exports various status files to the output folder path. When applicable, the exported files include processing files such as error lists, reconciliation reports, and success logs. 
- On the Job Configuration page, click  Edit Job . 
- Click the SFTP settings toggle, and enter your SFTP settings:
- SFTP Location – Select from the menu.
 Note : To configure the list of SFTP locations, you must use Extractor. For details on setting up an sFTP location, see  Configure External File Locations for Imports . 
- Folder Location to Retrieve Files – If necessary, enter the extension to the path described in the SFTP Location to import files from.  When left blank, Ingestor uses the SFTP location's root folder. 
- Global Input Folder Toggle – Sets the selected SFTP location as a global import folder. When multiple import jobs use the same SFTP Input File Path, Ingestor can identify the appropriate jobs and transfer the corresponding files from the file names.
 Note : If you want to use an SFTP location as a global location in Ingestor, then you must configure the SFTP location for global use on the SFTP location configuration screen. You cannot mark an SFTP location as global in Ingestor if it is not set as global on the SFTP location configuration screen. 
- Folder Location for Output Status Files  –  If necessary, enter the extension to the path described in the SFTP Location to save process files to.  When left blank, Ingestor uses the SFTP location's root folder. 
- Click Save Changes . 
## Toggle Import Type
 For external EDC jobs, Ingestor processes the imported files depending if the job's import type is cumulative or incremental.  
- On the Job Configuration page, click  Edit Job . 
- Select an Import Type: 
- Cumulative – Imported files replace all existing data. 
- Incremental – Imported files add to or update existing data. You can   Upsert – add or update existing items – or Delete items.   The uploaded file's name determines if Ingestor upserts or deletes items. If the file's name contains "upsert", then upsertion occurs. If the file's name contains "delete", then deletion occurs. 
- Click Save Changes .

===== PAGE: update_study_design_metadata_for_clinical_data_loading.html | Update Study Design Metadata for Clinical Data Loading =====
## Update Study Design Metadata for Clinical Data Loading
 You can update an external EDC job's data integration version to use updated metadata files. To do so, you must first load a new set of Study Design Metadata (SDM) files to the corresponding SDM import job to create a new data integration version. Then you can update the external EDC job to use that new data integration version. 
 For details about loading clinical data for CTMS, see Import Non-Rave Data for Medidata CTMS . 
## Create New Data Integration Version
- Prepare the required four SDM files for data loading: 
- Schedule of Assessments (SOA) 
- Visits 
- Data Sets 
- Fields
 Note : Metadata files must be cumulative. The uploaded file overwrites the corresponding metadata for the corresponding study. For details SDM file requirements, see Study Design Metadata File Standards . 
- On Ingestor’s Import Jobs screen, select an SDM import job. 
- Import the prepared metadata files into the SDM job to update the job’s data integration version. 
 The  data integration version number automatically increments to the next number. For example, if the latest version was 1.0, then the updated data integration version is 2.0. 
## Update External EDC Job to Use the New Data Integration Version
- Return to the Import Jobs screen, and select the External EDC import job that requires an updated data integration version. 
- Click the job’s Job Configuration tab. 
- On the job’s Job Configuration tab, click Edit Job . 
- Select the new data integration version from the updated list.
 Note : Ingestor’s cache takes about five minutes to reset. Therefore, it takes at least five minutes for a newly added version number to appear. 
- Click Save Changes . 
## Load Clinical Data Files with the Updated Data Integration Version
- Prepare clinical data files for data loading.
 Note : The prepared clinical data files must be cumulative since the Ingestor must fully refresh the datasets for the new data integration version.  
- In Ingestor, while still on the job’s Job Configuration tab, click the Job Files tab. 
- Import the prepared clinical data files into the external EDC file.
 Note : When l oading clinical data files cumulatively, Ingestor deletes all clinical data and metadata from the previous data integration version. Because of this deletion and reuploading process, data loading can take longer than usual.

===== PAGE: updated_counts_for_external_edc_data_files.html | Updated Counts for External EDC Data Files =====
## Updated Counts for External EDC Data Files
## Description
 When reviewing the import job summary for External EDC import jobs, the Import Summary panel >  Data Processing section shows the following record counts: 
- Updated – The number of rows in the imported file that resulted in updated data. 
- No Changes – The number of rows in the imported file that resulted in no data changes compared to the previous file import. 
- Deleted – The number of rows in the imported file that resulted in deleted data. 
- Successful  – The number of rows in the imported file that resulted in created data. 
- Failed –  The number of rows in the imported file that failed to be created, updated, or deleted.   
 Feature/enhancement stories referenced in this article:
 MCC-746608 
## Affected Roles
 All platform roles with one of the following building blocks:
- View External EDC Data 
- Import External EDC 
- Manage External EDC Data 
## Advantages
 With the updated record counts, you can track the changes your imported files causes with greater precision. That precision affords you more details when auditing or troubleshooting needs arise.
## User Impact
 You can find the updated record counts while reviewing External EDC jobs' Job Files page> Import Summary panel> Data Processing section. This update retroactively affects the import summaries of your previously created jobs as well. 
## Knowledge Space Updates
 The MEDS Ingestor Knowledge Space provides updated content to support this enhancement: 
- Track Progress for Clinical Data Import Jobs (updated) 
## Special Considerations
 None.

===== PAGE: updated_job_status_reporting_for_delayed_jobs.html | Updated Job Status Reporting for Delayed Jobs =====
## Updated Job Status Reporting for Delayed Jobs
## What Is New?
 The Medidata system's import job processor terminates jobs that take longer than six hours to process a job's first record. Prior to this update, Ingestor's job status screen labelled jobs terminated in this way as "Data Processing Failed".
 However with this update, Ingestor now presents jobs terminated in this way with the status "Processing completed, but there is a data delay".
 Feature/enhancement stories referenced in this article:
 MCC-1196222 
## What Is the Impact?
## Users/Roles
 All.
## Workflow Process
 No impact to the workflow process.
## Other Applications
 No impact to other applications.
## How Do I Benefit?
 This change brings more clarity to the status of your import jobs, especially edge cases.
## Where Do I See the Change?
 You can see the update on a job's Job Files tab. The new status appears under that screen's Status column.
## Where Do I Learn More?
 There are no additional relevant resources.
## What Else Should I Know?
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the  Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: updated_names_for_import_summary_artifact_files.html | Updated Names for Import Summary Artifact Files =====
## Updated Names for Import Summary Artifact Files
## Description
 When you download artifact files from any import job's Import Summary panel, the file names now follow the following convention: 
- <DataType>_<Name of Artifact>_<RunID>_<FileUploadDateTime>_<FileCompletionDateTime>.csv 
 Feature/enhancement stories referenced in this article:
 MCC-786582 
## Affected Roles
 All.
## Advantages
 The updated artifact file names make it easier for you to identify which file contains which information without needing to open the file.
## User Impact
 You can download any available artifact files from any job's Import Summary panel. You can see the updated file name while saving the file to your local device. The files' content are unchanged. 
## Knowledge Space Updates
 None.
## Special Considerations
 None.

===== PAGE: updates_to_import_jobs_list.html | Updates to Import Jobs List =====
## Updates to Import Jobs List
## Description
 The Import Jobs list screen includes the Status column that shows the status of a given import job. Statuses include:
- Active – The job is active, and you can upload files with the job. 
- Inactive – The study associated with the job is inactive, and you cannot upload files with the job. 
- Completed – The job is Completed, and you cannot upload files with the job. 
 In addition, the Import Level column now shows:
- The name of import job's target client division or study. 
- If the import job is at the CDS Level or at the Study Level. 
 You can filter import jobs by the Status or by the Import Level.
 Feature/enhancement stories referenced in this article:
 MCC-836591, MCC-867036 
## Affected Roles
 All.
## Advantages
 With the updated Job List screen, you can see more comprehensive data about your import jobs and identify the jobs you are interested in.
## User Impact
 See Description.
## Knowledge Space Updates
 None.
## Special Considerations
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: updates_to_import_jobs_page.html | Updates to Import Jobs Page =====
## Updates to Import Jobs Page
## Description
 MEDS Ingestor updates the Import Jobs page with the following columns:
- Created by – Shows the full name of the user that created the job. 
- Last Run Status – Shows the date and status of the last file that MEDS Ingestor uploaded or transferred. 
- Import Level – Denotes if the job is at the client division level or the study level. Formerly was the Study column. 
 In addition, the Import Jobs page now lists common column headers before listing column headers specific to data types, such as Data Integration|Version. The page includes an Additional Job Details panel containing specific information. The page also has a new default sorting order: your jobs with error alerts > your jobs without error alerts > other jobs in descending order by Last Run Status > other jobs with no uploaded files.
 Feature/enhancement stories referenced in this article:
 MCC-794828, MCC-795110, MCC-796570, MCC-808237, MCC-825199, MCC-827458 
## Affected Roles
 All.
## Advantages
 With the updated Import Jobs page, you can review your jobs faster with its more intuitive grouping and address issues as necessary. 
## User Impact
 See Description.
## Knowledge Space Updates
 None.
## Special Considerations
 To view Medidata testing documentation for this release, refer to the Medidata Validation Portal .

===== PAGE: view_details_for_processing_job_files.html | View Details For Processing Job Files =====
## View Details For Processing Job Files
## Description
 While reviewing an import job's Job Files tab the panel now groups processing details as a single processing job instead of showing details for each uploaded file. Grouped processing details include: 
- Status – The processing job's status. 
- Start Time – The processing job's start date and time.  
- End Time – The processing job's end date and time. 
- Imported by – The full name of the user that started the processing job. 
- No. of Records  – A link that opens the Import Summary panel. The Import Summary panel contains details about the individual processed files. 
 Feature/enhancement stories referenced in this article:
 MCC-794829, MCC-810463, MCC-819365 
## Affected Roles
 All.
## Advantages
 The updated Job Files tab streamlines the details that appear about your processed import job files, or processing jobs. With the streamlined tab, you can easily see common information about your latest processing jobs and make decisions without needing to delve into the details about each file.
## User Impact
 See Description.
## Knowledge Space Updates
 The MEDS Ingestor Knowledge Space provides updated content to support this enhancement: 
- Track Progress for CTMS Jobs 
- Track Progress for Clinical Data Import Jobs 
- Track Progress for Study Design Metadata Jobs 
- Track Progress for Study Master Data Jobs
